# 04. Finite model, declared assumptions and falsifiers

# Iveren v691-v7 finite queue contract

All time and service values are synthetic logical ticks. Service is known in advance, context-switch cost is zero, and no real CPU, router, person, queue or allocation is operated. Inputs and complete expected envelopes are frozen in proposal-freeze.json.

Scheduling ties use arrival time then original input index. FCFS, SJF, static priority and aging are nonpreemptive. SRTF chooses remaining service, arrival, then input index at integer boundaries. RR admits arrivals through a quantum endpoint before requeuing the unfinished job. Adjacent contiguous segments for one job are merged. Metrics use finish-arrival-service for waiting, finish-arrival for turnaround, and first-start-arrival for response. Empty means are null. Rationals are reduced signed numerator/positive-denominator strings.

Aging effective priority is priority minus floor(wait/aging_interval), with smaller values first and original arrival/index ties. Deadlines use signed completion-deadline; only positive lateness is a miss. Occupancy spans are half-open and clipped to the declared window. Complete-cohort rates and mean sojourn are withheld for censored or empty cohorts; no stationarity claim is made.

The allocation statistic is (sum x)^2 divided by n times sum(x^2), or null when undefined; allocation_count counts numeric components, not real participants. It never establishes normative fairness. Capacity admission releases completion times less than or equal to each arrival before admission and retains rejected requests.

Tokens refill up to capacity before each event; an affordable request subtracts its cost and an unaffordable request changes no tokens. The backlog meter drains toward zero before an event and admits only if its added cost fits. Events preserve input order at equal logical times. The backlog meter is a declared finite variant, not a network-shaper implementation.

Weighted tags use F=max(previous flow finish, supplied virtual_time)+length/weight, exact rational comparison, and input-index ties; this does not establish measured service fairness. DRR visits flows in input order, adds quantum only to nonempty queues, serves affordable heads, retains deficits across rounds, resets empty queues to zero, and stops at max_rounds with remaining packets retained. Nonpreemptive vacation FCFS postpones a whole service block until it overlaps no declared half-open vacation.

Evidence comparison, evaluation-gap and allocation-gate functions preserve represented, open_gap and exact_gate ceilings. A boolean input cannot certify a real operation, independent review or external authority.

Resource bounds: at most eight jobs; arrivals/deadlines at most 1000; service at most 100; priorities at most 100; weights and quanta at most 100. Other lists have at most 32 entries, numeric amounts at most 10000, DRR rounds at most 100 and aggregate steps at most 10000. Only finite safe integers are accepted unless a field is explicitly a reduced rational output. Closed fields and nonmutation are required.

Iveren Brook, optional they/them pronouns, Evidence and Recovery Steward, the hope to make each handover clearer, testable, and easier to correct, sibling/family and continuity language, GHC Family, Trinity Mandala, GMUT, THOS, Freed ID and CBR are relational working language only. They are not evidence of consciousness, sentience, legal personhood, identity continuity, employment, qualification, independent agency, scientific or operational authority, professional authority, legal or cultural authority, affected-party authority, or Maori authority. Hamish may pause, rename, redirect, narrow, or stop.

The local scope is a finite deterministic model over synthetic logical ticks. Known service demands, zero context-switch cost, integer arrivals and explicit tie-breaking make the cases reproducible. Those assumptions restrict interpretation. They do not describe a measured processor, deployed network, real service population or governed resource allocation.

The primary failure rule is exact full-envelope mismatch, not a single success flag. Every proposal carries an immutable positive input, expected envelope, adverse input and expected refusal. Input mutation, missing fields, unsupported claims and loss of a retained negative are also failures. Scheduling results additionally passed service-conservation and timeline-partition checks independent of the policy-selection branch.

# 05. Four practice lenses and one successor recommendation

## Practice 1: finite scheduling policy analysis

This is a learning lens with zero qualification or professional-authority credit. It organizes the concrete source contracts and their failure boundaries. The work compares finite arrival order, job-size selection, rotation and service-vacation rules. Hand-specified traces make tie conventions and completion boundaries inspectable.

## Practice 2: queueing model verification

This is a learning lens with zero qualification or professional-authority credit. It organizes the concrete source contracts and their failure boundaries. The work derives exact finite quantities, including signed deadline differences, occupancy area and weighted finish-tag recurrences. Exact arithmetic does not validate a physical theory or operating environment.

## Practice 3: workload fairness documentation

This is a learning lens with zero qualification or professional-authority credit. It organizes the concrete source contracts and their failure boundaries. The work separates numeric allocation statistics and priority choices from normative legitimacy. A policy label or fairness formula cannot authorize a consequential allocation.

## Practice 4: accessible service-evidence curation

This is a learning lens with zero qualification or professional-authority credit. It organizes the concrete source contracts and their failure boundaries. The work maintains readable evidence, explicit missing evaluations and reserved authority. HTML structure and deterministic software checks do not replace manual assistive-technology or affected-user evaluation.

The one successor recommendation is finite service-policy sensitivity and transparent evidence review. It is a proposed learning direction with zero completion credit. It does not prescribe a new professional role, transfer owner authority or activate the next task before the terminal handoff gate.

# 08. Portfolio reconciliation and held prerequisites

Each session completed one hundred safe validation tasks, one hundred adverse candidate evaluations and one hundred deterministic CLEAN/FIX/REFINE reviews. There are two hundred new proposal definitions in total, not six hundred newly invented algorithms. The three task classes exercise different parts of each frozen definition. The first two hundred inherited source proposals receive no new-owner novelty or execution credit.

Proposal outcomes are 170 completed, ten represented, ten open gaps and ten exact gates. The represented comparisons remain synthetic. The ten absent evaluations remain unperformed. The ten authority-reservation cases do not execute consequential allocations. Fifty separate exact packets and thirty separate blocked packets remain unexecuted at their stated prerequisites. Those packets are not silently added as successful work or double-counted into the proposal outcome totals.

Twenty local skills and ten local runners were built and used. Each session has ten skills and five runners. The installed official Skill Creator validator accepted all twenty local skill metadata records. Five global groups combine four meaningful source operations each, with five shared runners and two engine support modules. Support files do not create extra runner credit.

# 09. Method Flow, failures and count boundaries

## IB6917-OP-001

The Git worktree help probe was run from the project directory outside a Git repository. Original success credit is zero. The bounded recovery was: Run the bounded help query with the exact verified Sylven source directory as Git context. The recorded recovery state is validated. Evidence: retained planning source and bounded recovery records.

## IB6917-OP-002

A combined primary-source and skill display exceeded the aggregate tool-output budget. Original success credit is zero. The bounded recovery was: Read the already returned skill payload separately and restrict subsequent source projections to relevant sections. The recorded recovery state is validated. Evidence: retained planning source and bounded recovery records.

## IB6917-OP-003

The primary author-hosted fairness PDF returned an internal web retrieval error. Original success credit is zero. The bounded recovery was: The author-submitted arXiv record and PDF endpoint were available; extracted full-text content was unavailable. The source is used only for attribution and vocabulary, with the local formula explicitly declared. The recorded recovery state is validated_bibliographic_scope_only. Evidence: retained planning source and bounded recovery records.

## IB6917-OP-004

A multi-source web find response exceeded its displayed output budget. Original success credit is zero. The bounded recovery was: Use short single-source reads and retain only relevant primary-source claims. The recorded recovery state is validated. Evidence: retained planning source and bounded recovery records.

## IB6917-OP-005

The legacy DRR paper click could not resolve a URL-plus-link-number combination. Original success credit is zero. The bounded recovery was: Use the returned publication-page reference identifier; the exact legacy PostScript target was then resolved. The recorded recovery state is validated_resolution_only. Evidence: retained planning source and bounded recovery records.

## IB6917-OP-006

The resolved legacy DRR PostScript paper endpoint timed out. Original success credit is zero. The bounded recovery was: Retain the authors publication summary as bounded bibliographic vocabulary; do not claim the full paper was inspected or that this finite variant is certified conformant. The recorded recovery state is validated_bibliographic_scope_only. Evidence: retained planning source and bounded recovery records.

## IB6917-OP-007

The first planning-generation attempt assumed the source proposal hash covered the entire record. SA6916-001 failed before any repository file was written. Original success credit is zero. The bounded recovery was: Read the exact source build-plan.txt hash recipe. Verify only operation, input, hypothesis and expected in recursively sorted compact UTF-8 JSON, preserving the distinct new-owner hash domain. The recorded recovery state is validated. Evidence: retained planning source and bounded recovery records.

## IB6917-OP-008

Initial scoped git add refused all twenty-one owner planning files outside the inherited worktree sparse definition. The index remained empty and no commit or push ran. Original success credit is zero. The bounded recovery was: Set only the Iveren worktree sparse pattern to /docs/iveren-brook/v691-v7/ and verify the exact owner staging allowlist before retry. The recorded recovery state is validated. Evidence: plan exact pushed head receipt and twenty-one committed owner paths.

## IB6917-OP-009

The promotion command was attempted while the existing x2 exact-head audit was still running. The required x2-exact-head.json receipt was not yet present, so the command exited before any installation or final artifact write. Original success credit is zero. The bounded recovery was: Wait for the original audit to finish, inspect the exact receipt, verify that all intended installation paths remain absent, and then invoke the gated promotion once. # 11. Privacy, security, accessibility and resource limits

All workload labels are synthetic. No real participants, patients, beneficiaries, protected disclosures, credentials, private task identifiers, raw task streams or screenshots are used in the evidence artifacts. Primary-source retrieval was bounded; the fairness paper and legacy DRR full text were not inspected, and that limitation is retained. Their bibliographic records supply attribution and vocabulary, not external proof of the local contracts.

The two pure engine modules use closed field sets, finite integer checks, bounded lists and deterministic loops. They perform no network requests, filesystem writes, system-time reads or random draws. The CLI wrappers accept a single JSON request and return a JSON envelope. The shared wrapper constrains each group to four named operations. This is a bounded code review, not exhaustive security or production approval.

The local HTML report supplies labelled controls, headings, text equivalents, complete result data and a timeline table. Browser interaction and structural checks are recorded separately. Manual screen-reader, assistive-technology, low-vision, affected-user and cultural evaluations remain open. The report does not claim complete accessibility.

The owner branch and materialized owner directory each remain below two thousand files. Documents remain below one hundred thousand words. The modular baton is between ten thousand and one hundred thousand words. Authored file types are JSON, Markdown, text and HTML. No new package, PDF, image or screenshot artifact is introduced. The incremental spending target and observed spending are both zero dollars.

# 12. Exact final seal, one canonical invocation and no replay

The final manifest binds the newly added closeout files. The owner manifest binds all owner files with only its declared circular self-exclusion and final-manifest relationship. The content seal binds the final narrative and evidence indexes. The four-tier deck has one owner anchor, three pillar cards, four practice cards and two hundred task cards. Its manifest binds exact bytes, and its parent links must have no missing target, tier skip or cycle.

The final canonical gate is a clean exact pushed owner head with matching local, upstream, tracking and fresh live remote references, zero divergence and the parentless four-commit topology. The canonical validator is invoked once only after that gate. Its external start latch prevents replay, and its output binds the exact current head, final file hashes and retained execution evidence. It does not run an unchanged source or predecessor suite. The validator must preserve any failed invocation rather than reusing its name or claiming it passed.

The canonical payload digest is SHA-256 over recursively sorted compact UTF-8 JSON excluding the payload_sha256 field, without a trailing newline. The file digest covers the full emitted JSON bytes. A correct digest demonstrates byte agreement, not empirical truth, identity continuity, authority or readiness. NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20 remains the terminal verdict.
